- **ask-me**: Surface the open questions that are actually shaping or blocking the build path in THIS repo, and make me resolve them. Questions are derived from real repo state (ROADMAP/DECISIONS, TODO/FIXME/DEFER markers, in-flight branches, unratified assumptions in recent work) — never invented to fill a quota. Only load-bearing forks qualify: a different answer must change what gets built. Asks via AskUserQuestion chips with real, mutually exclusive options plus a Defer option whose consequence is stated. Caps at 3-4 per run, ranked by downstream unblocking. - **atman**: Atman is Vajra's learning loop. It reads real signals (tool failures in the practice log, failing verify-work runs, routing-log history) and writes **proposals only** to `~/.claude/vajra/proposals/`. Nothing changes until the user approves. ([thisizmsk-png/vajra](https://github.com/thisizmsk-png/vajra/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/atman.md) · ★2)
- **atom**: Namespace probe router command. ([girishmotwani/atom](https://github.com/girishmotwani/atom/blob/HEAD/poc/atom-short-namespace/fixture/com.github.copilot/commands/atom.md))
- **atom-audit**: Produce ATOM_AUDIT.md table auditing every atom component against COMPONENT_GUIDELINES.md (memo, callbacks, inline styles, touch target, a11y, reduced motion). Use when 2-4 weeks have passed since last audit, 5+ new atoms shipped, or COMPONENT_GUIDELINES has a new normative rule. Do not use when no atoms exist (run design-bootstrap first), for a single atom (use design-spec-review instead), or for 6 or more atoms (use atom-audit-fleet). ([Mozurok/fhorja.dev](https://github.com/Mozurok/fhorja.dev/blob/HEAD/commands/atom-audit.md) · ★10)
- **atom-audit-fleet**: Audit every atom in parallel against the component guidelines, one worker per small group, merging rows into one ATOM_AUDIT.md table (orchestrator-workers variant of atom-audit). Use when the atom count is 6 or more and COMPONENT_GUIDELINES.md exists. Do not use below 6 atoms (use atom-audit single-agent), without COMPONENT_GUIDELINES.md, or when only 1 or 2 atoms changed (use design-spec-review per component). ([Mozurok/fhorja.dev](https://github.com/Mozurok/fhorja.dev/blob/HEAD/commands/atom-audit-fleet.md) · ★10)
